Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the rooms space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 379 and the convergence rate is O(470/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to rooms theory, we decompose the problem into 379 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 470. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
